  ▶ TC-AU-01: record() writes the envelope in the caller transaction
    ✔ actor, realm, audience and organisation come from the scope, not from the caller (91.300689ms)
    ✔ rolls back with the caller: a failed business transaction leaves no audit row (9.744535ms)
    ✔ print, search, export, download and update actions are all accepted; the closed list refuses others (13.132893ms)
    ✔ envelope rules: reason required for export and BTG, request or job id required, secrets only as version ids (72.662757ms)
    ✔ RLS: another organisation, the anonymous audience and a missing scope cannot write (16.759327ms)
    ✔ concurrent writers do not wait on each other: 40 records over 6 connections all commit (174.948199ms)
  ✔ TC-AU-01: record() writes the envelope in the caller transaction (379.892403ms)
  ▶ TC-AU-02: an audit failure rolls the business change back

  ✔ TC-AU-02: an audit failure rolls the business change back (25.823538ms)
  ▶ TC-AU-03: the denial path

    ✔ verification of untouched data passes and reports the partition horizon (18.509647ms)

    ✔ BR-BE01-02: verification covers older anchored ranges, not just recent ones (37.466893ms)

  ✔ TC-AU-08: anchoring and verification (670.966132ms)
  ▶ TC-AU-07: the export job is chunked, size limited and itself audited

▶ notifications on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-06, worker side)
  ▶ the send job
    ✔ e-mail: renders the published version in the person's language, attaches the .ics, stores the vendor reference and no address or text (176.296086ms)
    ✔ language: the person's language when published, else the default; SMS and push render without a subject/with a title (36.39408ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-08: SMS off and e-mail on - the SMS is suppressed with its reason and the provider is never called; e-mail is sent (53.243251ms)
    ✔ suppression reasons: no address, unknown recipient, inactive patient, no contributor, no template (35.543985ms)
    ✔ in-app notices need no vendor and are sent even when every external channel is off (TC-NT-04) (8.63477ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-04: a failing provider is retried by the queue; the failure is an error CLASS; the last attempt publishes delivery_failed; recovery sends with the same token (75.302919ms)
    ✔ a vendor refusal (4xx) is final: failed, no queue retry, delivery_failed published (12.599738ms)
    ✔ quiet hours defer the send to when they end, restore the attempt count and queue the job (29.671748ms)
    ✔ a template that leaks text is stopped at send time by the output scan; the provider is never called (36.783692ms)
    ✔ a render error (a declared value missing) is a final failure, not a retry (8.040094ms)
    ✔ BE06-F1: a merge that re-points the row during the vendor call - sent, failed and deferred outcomes are still recorded (under the survivor) (49.307026ms)
    ✔ a patient merged while the job waited: the job follows the survivor and sends to the survivor's contacts (on_merged = resolve) (54.151636ms)
    ✔ a staff notice runs under the organisation scope and resolves through the principal contributor (7.289777ms)
    ✔ no address, message text or canary reaches a log line (0.178695ms)
  ✔ the send job (585.217318ms)
  ▶ delivery receipts (33.1 notifications.on_receipt, SBR-16)
    ✔ a receipt for a kept notification of a merged patient attaches to the SURVIVOR; a repeat of the event is a no-op (22.968693ms)
    ✔ untrusted payloads: unknown reference, bad shape, bad status, oversized and ambiguous references record nothing (10.083207ms)
    ✔ the inbound handler records through the same path (40.763238ms)
    ✔ the worker role cannot update or delete a receipt, or write one for a patient outside its job scope (4.761903ms)
  ✔ delivery receipts (33.1 notifications.on_receipt, SBR-16) (80.78843ms)
  ▶ channel adapters implement the BE-02 interface
    ✔ argument checks reject header injection, malformed addresses and oversize text without echoing them (0.664286ms)
    ✔ provider errors map to AdapterFailure with the provider's retryability; any other exception is retryable; health never throws (0.382728ms)
  ✔ channel adapters implement the BE-02 interface (1.171187ms)
